2026-11-20T17:33:59.0068462Z [36;1m# rule in verify/ and deleted the entries the new rule would reject[0m

2026-11-20T17:33:59.0072257Z [36;1m# two narrower steps run in its place: the tests marked `board`,[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0072606Z [36;1m# which read the committed data as a whole, and the tests that name[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0072932Z [36;1m# an entry it changed (issue #2940), computed below. Anything else[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0073213Z [36;1m# changed -> the whole suite runs.[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0073409Z [36;1m#[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0073623Z [36;1m# The rule used to list codes/, circuits/, notes/ and fieldnotes/[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0073946Z [36;1m# and to require a codes/ or circuits/ change, which was two entries[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0074267Z [36;1m# short of what the scope check lets a submission carry. So a PR[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0074585Z [36;1m# that added a certificate, beside its entry or alone, ran every[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0074908Z [36;1m# self-test: about an hour of CI per PR, for a suite in which a[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0075245Z [36;1m# few tests open anything under certs/. The certificate checker is[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0075565Z [36;1m# not among the things skipped. It is its own step below and runs[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0075819Z [36;1m# on every event.[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0076005Z [36;1m#[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0076220Z [36;1m# A diff that cannot be computed exits non-zero, which the first[0m

2026-11-20T17:33:59.0076845Z [36;1m# the data, so the suite runs (fail closed). $DATA_SURFACE and[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0077161Z [36;1m# $outside are unquoted so that they split into one pathspec per[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0077411Z [36;1m# entry.[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0077579Z [36;1moutside=""[0m

2026-11-20T17:33:59.0078196Z [36;1mif ! git diff --quiet "$base"...HEAD -- $DATA_SURFACE && git diff --quiet "$base"...HEAD -- $outside; then[0m

2026-11-20T17:33:59.0079043Z [36;1mfi[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0079372Z [36;1m# Skipping the suite for such a PR is right about the stack, but a[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0079729Z [36;1m# self-test can name an entry by slug, and issue #2940 is what that[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0080192Z [36;1m# cost: #2901 tightened codes/646-162-26.json while[0m

2026-11-20T17:33:59.0081522Z [36;1m# surfaced only with the next PR that touched a test. The slug is[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0081846Z [36;1m# right there in the test source, so ask the suite which files[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0082233Z [36;1m# mention an entry this PR changed and run exactly those: a grep[0m

2026-11-20T17:33:59.0082958Z [36;1m# #2901 on #2901 itself. Only the slugs come from the PR's diff;[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0083283Z [36;1m# the files it can name are this tree's own tests, filtered to[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0083583Z [36;1m# that shape below before anything reaches pytest.[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0083826Z [36;1m#[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0084065Z [36;1m# A certificate and a repro manifest are named for their entry too[0m

2026-11-20T17:33:59.0084731Z [36;1m# and a test can pin what one of them says, so their slugs are read[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0084998Z [36;1m# the same way.[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0085184Z [36;1m#[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0085579Z [36;1m# The shape filter used to admit only a test directly under one of[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0085988Z [36;1m# the four directories. research/ has since grown subdirectories[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0086411Z [36;1m# (audits/, certs/, cost/, local2d/, provenance/) that hold 17 test[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0086822Z [36;1m# files, most of which name entries, and every one of them was[0m

2026-11-20T17:33:59.0087865Z [36;1m#[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0088120Z [36;1m# A test can also pin an entry without naming it. The validation[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0088544Z [36;1m# gate (verify/validation_gate.py) keeps a golden verdict for each[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0088969Z [36;1m# of its published reproductions under $GATE_GOLDENS, named for the[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0089353Z [36;1m# entry, and verify/test_validation_gate.py fails when[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0089690Z [36;1m# codes/<slug>.json no longer reproduces it. The[0m

2026-11-20T17:33:59.0090640Z [36;1m# the test's source, so the grep cannot see them: #5929 changed the[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0091022Z [36;1m# circuit tier of codes/160-36-6.json, passed here, and the gate[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0091437Z [36;1m# test then failed on every pull request that ran the suite until[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0091821Z [36;1m# #6241 re-recorded the golden. A changed entry that has a golden[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0092176Z [36;1m# therefore selects the gate's test file as well.[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0092497Z [36;1mslug_tests=""[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0092886Z [36;1mslugs=$(git diff --name-status --diff-filter=AMDRT "$base"...HEAD -- codes circuits certs repro \[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0093316Z [36;1m          | cut -f2- | tr '\t' '\n' \[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0093611Z [36;1m          | sed -n -e 's|^codes/\(.*\)\.json$|\1|p' \[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0093982Z [36;1m                   -e 's|^circuits/\([^/]*\)/.*|\1|p' \[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0094303Z [36;1m                   -e 's|^certs/\(.*/\)\{0,1\}\([^/]*\)\.json$|\2|p' \[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0094710Z [36;1m                   -e 's|^repro/\([^/]*\)\.json$|\1|p' | sort -u)[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0095035Z [36;1mfor slug in $slugs; do[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0095370Z [36;1m  # Both spellings of one entry: the slug the file is named for,[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0095765Z [36;1m  # and the [[n,k,d]] form a test may cite instead. -F keeps a[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0096128Z [36;1m  # slug that looks like a pattern (and one that starts with a[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0096485Z [36;1m  # dash) from meaning anything to grep.[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0096825Z [36;1m  slug_tests="$slug_tests $(grep -rlF --include='test_*.py' \[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0097155Z [36;1m                -e "$slug" -e "${slug//-/,}" \[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0097540Z [36;1m                research verify site cli || true)"[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0097875Z [36;1m  if [ -f "$GATE_GOLDENS/$slug.json" ]; then[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0098202Z [36;1m    slug_tests="$slug_tests verify/test_validation_gate.py"[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0098506Z [36;1m  fi[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0098731Z [36;1mdone[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0099005Z [36;1mslug_tests=$(printf '%s\n' $slug_tests \[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0099402Z [36;1m               | grep -E '^(research|verify|site|cli)/([A-Za-z0-9_]+/)*test_[A-Za-z0-9_.]+\.py$' \[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0099791Z [36;1m               | sort -u | tr '\n' ' ' | sed 's/ *$//' || true)[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0100274Z [36;1mif [ -n "$slug_tests" ]; then[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0100674Z [36;1m  echo "self-tests naming an entry this PR changed: $slug_tests" | tee -a "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY"[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0101079Z [36;1mfi[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0101359Z [36;1m# A circuit.ler claim nothing in the PR touched can only go stale[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0101786Z [36;1m# through the stack that measures it: the measured-rate tier, the[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0102198Z [36;1m# decoder, the pinned dependencies (a stim bump in uv.lock) or the[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0102577Z [36;1m# accelerator build, which is the LER_STACK list in this job's env.[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0103021Z [36;1m# A PR that leaves all of those alone gets the --ler-base run; a PR[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0103403Z [36;1m# that touches any of them re-measures a sample of the claims[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0103788Z [36;1m# before it merges, and the weekly runs re-measure them all (the[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0104225Z [36;1m# sweep that passes --ler-sample below has the argument). Everything else[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0104628Z [36;1m# under verify/ (the structural verifier, the circuit tier, the[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0105009Z [36;1m# gate, the policy checkers, tests, fixtures, the integrity[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0105388Z [36;1m# manifest) runs on every entry in the --ler-base pass as well and[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0105811Z [36;1m# cannot change a measured rate, so it is left out. The list used[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0106199Z [36;1m# to be all of verify/ minus tests, checkers, and fixtures: a[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0106585Z [36;1m# prose-checker edit scheduled the full re-measure on PR #3418, and[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0107010Z [36;1m# once those were excluded a schema-check edit did on #4653 and[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0107370Z [36;1m# #4682, which with about an hour of self-tests runs past this[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0107763Z [36;1m# job's 130-minute timeout. A diff that cannot be computed exits[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0108157Z [36;1m# non-zero and so counts as changed (fail closed). $LER_STACK is[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0108556Z [36;1m# unquoted so that it splits into one pathspec per entry.[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0108907Z [36;1mif git diff --quiet "$base"...HEAD -- $LER_STACK; then[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0109267Z [36;1m  ler_stack_changed=false[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0109537Z [36;1melse[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0109755Z [36;1m  ler_stack_changed=true[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0110176Z [36;1mfi[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0110441Z [36;1mecho "codes_changed=$codes_changed" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:59.0110900Z [36;1mecho "data_only=$data_only" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"[0m
